### Broken or false

**Snapshots and Jobs**
- **Shared containers are rejected as cycles.** In `kzen-lib/.../exec/data/snapshot/DataSnapshot.kt`, `seenIdentities` (:126) is never removed (:181), so the check at :178 fails any object graph that reaches the same container twice. `openIdentities` (:125) is popped at :200 but never read. Through `EngineJobControl.kt:312-320` this fails Jobs. The scan is also O(n²).
- **The default snapshot caps fail Jobs.** `SnapshotPolicy.kt:13-19` sets the caps and `EngineJobControl.kt:312-320` throws on `Rejected`, contradicting 2026-10-09 12:26. architecture.md presents "bounded snapshots" as design.
- **LogicParameter defaults vanish silently.** A default that fails the snapshot becomes Optional with no error (kzen-auto LogicParameter.kt:61-67).
- **The adapter error gives impossible advice.** "Register an explicit adapter" (DefaultDataAdapterRegistry.kt:396-405) cannot be followed: the production registry is empty and has no registration hook.

**Code that never runs**
- **Branches that never run in practice:** `SnapshotResult.Redacted` (FlowRun.kt:749, ScriptRunContext.kt:596, EngineJobControl.kt:318) and the SchemaDiagnostic UI (DataContractPresentation.kt:54).
- **An empty close step.** `RunEngine.kt:569-580` `closeDataAdapters()` has an empty body (`if (close) { }`) and has done nothing since 2026-08-29.

**Engine and trace**
- **Trace time is query time.** `kzen-auto/.../server/exec/RunEngineLogicTrace.kt:129, 157, 189` stamps `Clock.System.now()` when the trace is queried, and `TraceEvent` (kzen-lib-common/.../exec/engine/TraceEvent.kt:17) has no time field. The KDoc's "when it was recorded" is false, and this fails the 2026-06-17 15:22 request.
- **objectStableId is the document id.** `RunEngineLogicTrace.kt:194-195` sets it to the document node id, so the screenshot film strip (PageScreenshots.kt:115) is titled by document, not by step. Not confirmed in a running UI.
- **The `retained` comment is false in the product.** RunEngine.kt:262-266 and RetainedBinding.kt say retention keeps bindings "findable and closeable", but only tests call `retainedBindings`/`releaseRetained`, and `dispose()` (:556-560) never closes them.
- **stepOut KDoc is wrong.** The LogicController `stepOut` KDoc says "deepest currently-paused frame", but the engine uses the shallowest (`minOf`, RunEngine.kt:447).
- **The raw-string layer has no users.** Execution.kt:243-250 and kzen-auto ScriptRunContext.kt:283-290 call it a SUPPORTED plugin interop path, but no plugin, step or sample uses it.
- **architecture.md's Execution row is incomplete.** It leaves out `pauseHere`, `recoverable`, `blocking`, `discardCaptured` and `removedStableIds`.

**YAML and notation**
- **The save fallback hides bugs.** `YamlNotationParser.kt:115-120` swallows any exception from the format-preserving save with no log, so a save bug shows up only as lost formatting.
- **"Self-extinguishing" is false**, both in YamlParser.kt:832-834 and in kzen-lib/docs/architecture.md:132. The format-preserving save (YamlNotationParser.kt:158-160) copies the old text of unchanged objects verbatim, so legacy backslashes are never rewritten.
- **architecture.md:132 is wrong about bare values with `:`.** It says a bare value such as a Windows path or `material-symbols:name` "mis-parses as a nested map, so quote it". In the code, a value after `key: ` runs literally to end of line (YamlParser.kt:375-376 "`test: a: b` is the scalar `a: b` and never nests"), and a colon not followed by a space or end of line is a plain scalar (:627-630).
- **"Degrades gracefully" is false for tags.** architecture.md:54 says broken notation degrades gracefully, but an invalid tag throws from the `ObjectTag` constructor (ObjectTag.kt:27) at NotationMetadataReader.kt:118, inside the uncaught `read()` (:35-57). Whether callers further up catch it is unverified. Failing loudly matches the user's rule; the doc claim does not.
- **ObjectStableMapper's bound claim is false on the client.** The comment at ObjectStableMapper.kt:16-20 says draining bounds `removedIds`, but JS never drains it.

**Stale docs and comments**
- **kzen-lib/AGENTS.md:25** names `GraphCreator.create()`; the methods are `createGraph` / `tryCreateGraph` (GraphCreator.kt:42, 113).
- **kzen-lib/AGENTS.md:69** says KSP tasks need `--no-configuration-cache`, but `gradle.properties:4-5` enables the configuration cache with problems as warnings. Unverified either way without a build. The jvmTarget-17 reason it gives is likely stale (C-16).
- **kzen-auto KzenAutoContext.kt:275-278** says two services are lazy providers; there are 8.

**Accurate (checked):** logic-spec.md's known-gaps block (lines 15-24); architecture.md:244-258 on the engine; the claims that the reflective mirror logs each class once, all four modules publish to mavenLocal, the processor writes nothing when there are no @Reflect classes, and digests are persisted across runs.

### Not covered

- **RunEngine internals:** `hostChild` (:988-1062, `migrationNodeWatermark`), `adoptRetiredFrames` / `supersedeRetiredFrames` and `restoredForNode` gating. Comments were read, not the full logic.
- **Exec values:** ExecutionRequest / ExecutionResult / RequestParams, and LogicTracePath internals (`$stable` / `$outcome` markers).
- **Data model:** correctness of DataTypeAlgebra / DataValueAlgebra, LiteralDataValues, TaggedUnionValueAccess, NativeClasses, most overlay files, ProducedBindingsBuilder, DataProblem codes, and DefaultNativeTypeResolver beyond line 140.
- **Runtime-only claims (unverified):** B-21 node-table cost and B-31 memo thread-safety. "Defaults applied once when executable inputs are bound" was not verified.
- **Notation, line level:** NotationEvent / NotationCommand, NotationReducerRefactor and NotationReducerAttributes (provenance only); DocumentPath / AttributePath / ObjectNesting / EscapedDelimiter correctness; the full FileNotationMedia diff; ClasspathNotationMedia, ReadWriteNotationMedia, WeakAttributeDefiner and AttributeObjectDefiner; GraphNotation `inheritanceChainCache` thread safety (pre-existing).
- **Docs and JS:** the body of docs/logic-spec.md (only known-gaps and headings were read), the kzen-lib-js ClientJsonUtils deletion, and the agent plan docs under kzen/docs/plans as provenance sources (agent-recorded, not user intent).
- **Other areas:** JobDeadlockMonitor grace and the Flow/Job emit throttles belong to the Job/Flow audits. The pre-May `Random(42)` seed in LogicExecutionId is the user's own and was not judged.
- **Not checked:** whether existing user project YAML (kzen-proj, user workspaces) actually contains backslash escapes inside single quotes (C-2) or `key: - x` (C-3).

## Area I: the smaller siblings (kzen-launcher, kzen-shell, kzen-project, kzen-sample, kzen-repo)

### Summary
- **What's here.** The shell/launcher/project trio is the desktop runtime: a reverse proxy, process spawning, the project/archetype registries, and the dist build. Since 2026-05-01 agents rewrote about 3.2k lines in kzen-shell, 4.4k in kzen-launcher and 1.2k in kzen-project. Almost all of it traces to one agent-written plan (`plans/2026-07-06_shell-launcher-project-improvements.md`) that the user approved as a whole ("I want to implement this plan. please start with Phase 1.", 2026-07-08 09:09; then "implement the next pre-planned work item, SH2/SH3/SH4", 2026-07-21). Distribution, the archetype properties list, build-info and the delete/remove buttons were requested directly.
- **kzen-sample** (~18k lines, almost all agent-written) holds `itch-plugin` (a Java core plus the kzen adapter) and `spring-embed` (a Spring Boot host plus a Gradle KMP frontend).
- **Missing record.** There are no user prompts and no transcripts between 2026-09-06 and 2026-09-14. That window produced the Nasdaq catalog/download, the Dated* workers, the Zstd store v2, store leases, prefetch and 90 duplicate getters, so their provenance cannot be established ("Not traced").
- **kzen-repo** received only the 0.29.1 release artifacts, as part of the requested release.
- **Worst problems:**
  1. The itch-plugin core and adapter still decide gzip and file type by reading bytes: `ItchFrameInput` checks the 0x1f8b magic, the ITCH content probe is "never by file name alone", and the world-cities reader detects its header row. This directly contradicts the user's 2026-10-12/10-14 requests, and spring-embed and its tests depend on it.
  2. Invented limits throughout:
     - shell: a 120 s child readiness kill (it also applies to the launcher, which downloads archetypes before it binds), a 60 s proxy socket deadline, a 10 MB child-log cap;
     - launcher: a 128-character project-name cap;
     - spring-embed: 5 s/30 s/6 h timeouts and a 4 GiB budget default;
     - itch-plugin core: a 512 MiB build budget, an 8 MiB/30 s prefetch, 1 MiB fingerprint samples.
  3. Test hooks ship in main code. spring-embed has unauthenticated `POST /kzen-host/shutdown` and `/kzen-host/budget/hold` (any page in the user's browser can trigger them). The launcher has a `ShellSimulator` mode in which project names containing "fail"/"exit" behave differently.
  4. Hand-rolled code where a library or the JDK exists: the spring-embed MVC proxy (Spring Cloud Gateway MVC), WeightedBudget (a fair `Semaphore`; the current one is not FIFO), PersistentSortedMap.
  5. One concrete hang: if the launcher fails to boot, the shell's window stays on "Loading..." forever.
  6. Several AGENTS/README claims are false, including the umbrella's "`useCommonJs()` must stay".

Rows: A1–A28 (shell/launcher/project/repo), S1–S32 (itch-plugin), E1–E29 (spring-embed): 89 rows. Section S and E rows were produced by two parallel sub-auditors and spot-checked (the `ItchFrameInput` magic-byte check, the prompt gap, the `/kzen-host/shutdown` and `hold` endpoints, and the WeightedBudget `wait`/`notifyAll` loop were all confirmed in code).

(2026-07-08 09:09). Later phases ran under "implement the next pre-planned work item, SH2/SH3/SH4" (2026-07-21 15:31 / 17:37 / 18:19) and "go ahead and start implementing the master plan" (2026-07-10 22:45). #### Broken or false (section A)
- **Shell stuck on "Loading..." when the launcher fails to boot.** In kzen-shell/src/main/kotlin/tech/kzen/shell/KzenShellMain.kt:28, `context.start()` runs outside the `try`. When `MainJarRunner.start` throws `MainJarProcessStartException`, the main thread dies, but the Swing window (shown at :78) stays on "Loading...". Causes: the launcher exits (e.g. `ProjectRepo` refuses an unparseable `kzen-projects.yaml`, ProjectRepo.kt:267-273), or it misses the 120 s readiness deadline while downloading archetypes before binding (A2). Neither the bind-failure pane nor any error is shown. (Inferred from code; not run.)
- **The readiness deadline does not bound the "alive but silent" case its comment claims.** In kzen-shell/.../util/ProcessAwaitUtil.kt:9-23, `isAvailable` uses `HttpURLConnection` with no connect or read timeout. A child that accepts the TCP connection but never answers blocks the poll forever, so the 120 s deadline (MainJarProcess.kt:21-23, "this only bounds the pathological alive-but-silent case") never fires.
- **False code comment** at kzen-shell/.../KzenShellMain.kt:74: "Launcher + project-archetype sources resolve via --args > kzen-shell.properties > GitHub default." There is no GitHub default: `KzenShellProperties.load` errors when the key is missing (KzenShellProperties.kt:53-56), and the shell has no project-archetype source at all.
- **kzen-shell/AGENTS.md is stale**:
  - "Everything else (GET/PUT/POST/GET-with-trailing-slash) falls through to `ProxyHandler.handle()`". The route is `route("{...}") { handle { ... } }`, which covers all methods (KzenShellMain.kt:157-161).
  - End-to-end step 3 says the launcher is registered "in `ProcessRegistry` as `main`". It is registered under its unpack-dir name (`KzenShellContext.kt:260-261`) and found by jar path.
- **kzen-launcher/AGENTS.md is stale**:
  - It lists `properties/KzenProperties.kt | Config & archetype defaults`. That file was deleted (directory absent).
  - It says the dist zip is "the layout kzen-shell's `ArtifactRepo` expects". The class was renamed `ArtifactInstaller`.
  - It says "`ArchetypeRepo.install` downloads to a `.part` file". The method is `acquire` (ArchetypeRepo.kt:73).
- **kzen-project/AGENTS.md is stale**: the Key-directories table lists `kzen-project-jvm/src/main/kotlin/tech/kzen/project/common/CommonServer.kt`, which was deleted (diff `b8fcc50309..HEAD`).
- **The umbrella kzen/AGENTS.md (Toolchain pins) is false**: it says "`useCommonJs()` must stay (`@mui/icons-material` is CommonJS-packaged and breaks under ESM resolution)". kzen-auto-js and kzen-launcher-js both use `useEsModules()` (kzen-launcher-js/build.gradle.kts:21-26 even says MUI 9 "unblocked this"). The same claim is repeated in kzen-auto/AGENTS.md:116 and kzen-auto/docs/js-architecture.md:356, :463.
- **Build-info deviates from the request** (minor): the user asked for a timestamp appended for snapshots. Releases get it too (BuildInfo.display, always-written `timestamp=`).
- **Plan text vs. code**: PLAN-07-08 phase 2 promised a "properties-overridable" 90 s start deadline. The code is a fixed 120 s with no property (MainJarRunner has only a constructor default).

#### Broken or false (section B)
- **ITCH reader doc vs. code.** The ItchReaderCapability doc says incoming bytes are "already inflated", but `ItchDataCursor.java:46` and `ItchReaderCapability.java:159` sniff for gzip again. A doubly-gzipped file is inflated twice.
- **Content sniffing is still live** (S1–S3) despite the requests on 2026-10-12 and 10-14.
- **README benchmark claim.** The README says the benchmark "writes a Markdown report under `<data>/reports/`". It only prints to stdout.
- **Stale README counts.** It says 5 workers, 7 @Reflect classes and 2 documents; there are 7, 9 and 3 (`dated-workers.yaml` is missing from both the README and PluginDirectoryIT).
- **Duplicate titles.** Two workers are both titled "ITCH trade volume".
- **Double delete.** `StoreVersionLease.prune` deletes the same path twice.
- **Dead code:** `readRecords`, `replay`/`stream`/`marketWide`, `Coefficients.initial`, `transientHeadroom`, `captureProvenance`.
- **PersistentSortedMap's stated reason is false.** Its doc says the core is dependency-free; the core depends on zstd-jni.
- **Unguarded limitation.** ItchStoreBuilder's doc admits that two concurrent builds of one store aren't supported (last pointer write wins), and nothing prevents it.

#### Broken or false (section C)
- **Unused services, false comment.** application.yaml:22 says the services are "offered to every workspace as @Service objects", but nothing anywhere consumes TradeRepository or OrderBookService.
- **pom.xml:37** says the overrides "must follow kzen's train", but kotlinx-serialization is 1.11.0 against kzen's 1.9.0.
- **AGENTS.md:21** omits the `kzen.host.{day-file,data-root,budget-bytes}` properties.
- **Shutdown and budget hold are exposed to the browser.** `/kzen-host/shutdown` and `/kzen-host/budget/hold` are unauthenticated CORS-simple POSTs, and `hold` can block a Tomcat thread with no timeout.
- **Possible starvation.** WeightedBudget is not FIFO (WeightedBudget.java:60-79).
- **Catalog polling is expensive** (see E20).
- **Two trade-volume algorithms.** `/host/trades` with and without a date compute the tally differently, and nothing checks that they agree.
- **`.part` downloads are never cleaned up** (ItchCatalog.java:101-104; README.md:67 admits it). Each can be several GB.
- **Test-only code in main.** `ItchCatalog.selected()` is used only by tests.
- **Unverified proxy justification.** AGENTS.md:101-103 claims "Tomcat buffers [StreamingResponseBody] until completion". That is unverified; StreamingResponseBody normally streams on flush, so the custom proxy may be working around an unidentified cause.
- **README.md:40 copies kzen-auto's Preview limits** (8 MiB, 256 KiB, 16, 200, 2,000, 4,096, 64 B, 50 ms). Only 16 / 2,000 / 4,096 were confirmed in PreviewCapture.kt:15-18. Duplicated numbers will drift.

### Not covered
- **Shell/launcher/project:** launcher JS components were read only for limits and polling (ProjectItem, NewProjectScreen and ProjectRunning layout were not reviewed line by line). Test bodies (ArchetypeRepoTest, ProjectCreatorUpgradeTest, ShellTestStub, ...) were not reviewed. The esbuild/npm-pin blocks in the `-js` build scripts were not reviewed (they are shared with kzen-auto, so that area's auditor covers them). RELEASING.md was only spot-checked. Nothing was run, so A2's slow-download kill and the "Loading..." hang are inferred from code.
- **itch-plugin:** not read in full: SymbolDayGraph past line 80, BookHistorySampler, OrderLifecycle, BookSnapshot, ItchMessage past line 80, ItchLayout, most test bodies. Also unchecked: whether the plugin compiles against current kzen-auto, and whether an uppercase `.NASDAQ_ITCH50.gz` matches the extension.
- **spring-embed:** test bodies were read only for structure and timeouts; frontend/kotlin-js-store/yarn.lock was not reviewed.
- **Out of area:** kzen-auto pieces (CursorSourceWorker hooks, KzenAutoHost, the "No progress while owned natives are held" stall warning, PreviewCapture limits).
- **Unknowable provenance:** pasted prompt bodies of 2026-07-06 15:21, 07-09 15:17/16:40 and 07-10 20:51 are not in prompts.md, and the 2026-09-06 → 09-14 gap cannot be recovered.

## Area J — Docs, agent guidance and process

**Summary.** Area J is the written layer agents work from. It covers the umbrella `AGENTS.md`, `docs/CODING_STANDARDS.md` (21 rules, 524 lines) and `docs/RELEASING.md`. It also covers 84 plan files (~20.9k lines), 10 analyses (~9.1k lines), 8 audits, each sibling's AGENTS.md and architecture docs (~4k lines), and 11 memory files.

Most of the *process* rules can be traced to the user: the audit, file-safety, staging, scratch-file and memory conventions, the release train, and most coding-standard rules.

The worst problem is in the *design* documents. Three of them make content sniffing the design authority:
- the data-format-detection analysis, which says "gzip magic bytes are authoritative";
- its 6-session plan, marked "complete";
- the earlier data-reading analysis, which says "renaming an identical object ... can[not] change interpretation".

A kzen-auto AGENTS.md gotcha also tells agents that "a binary format is detected by content". None of these trace to a prompt. The detection analysis was written and implemented outside the recorded sessions: commits `7d1339a4`/`6fd2ae30` on 2026-09-03 have no prompt between 2026-09-02 17:43 and 2026-09-03 21:56. The user's 2026-10-12 and 2026-10-14 statements contradict all of it, and none of these docs is marked superseded.

Second, plans record agent-chosen limits as decisions: 256 values, a 2.5 s editor deadline and a 30 s run deadline, "answered as defaults" with no user quote. Plans also call themselves "the only record" of rationale, which contradicts the user's 2026-07-10 "plans as ephemeral" instruction.

Third, a spot-check of names the docs cite against the code found 7 false factual claims (stale or nonexistent symbols, a removed feature still documented) and 3 stale ledger/lifecycle claims. The toolchain pins and build gotchas checked out true.

### Broken or false

F1-F7 are facts the code contradicts. F8-F10 are stale process claims. F11-F14 are docs that are true of the code but violate the user's principles or misreport provenance.

- **F1. `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:227`** says `File` puts "{name, path, size, modified} plus Name-pattern values as metadata (`FileValues`)". The Name pattern was deleted on 2026-10-13 (kzen-auto `cb908309`; user 16:23 "yes delete it"). `FileValues.kt:20-27,42-45` defines only name/path/size/modified (+parent). The 2026-10-13 docs commit `bea013c` updated three plan/analysis files but not this one.
- **F2. `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:675`** lists the context service `filterIndex` / `FilterIndex` ("Persistent column-value index backing report filtering"). No symbol `FilterIndex` or `filterIndex` exists in any kzen-auto `*.kt`.
- **F3. `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:113`** says Formula/output Workers "use exclusive `RecordOutputBuilder` claims". No `RecordOutputBuilder` exists in the source.
- **F4. `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:305`** cites `ScriptExecutionContext.referencedValue`. No such class exists. `referencedValue` is on `StepExecution` (`kzen-auto-jvm/.../objects/script/api/StepExecution.kt:61`) and is implemented in `ScriptRunContext.kt:134`.
- **F5. `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:782`** names "the test-only `CssSelectorTarget`". The actual class is `CssSelectorTargetType` (`kzen-auto-jvm/src/test/.../service/target/test/CssSelectorTargetType.kt`). Minor.
- **F6. `kzen-lib/docs/architecture.md:316`** lists `PlatformSynchronized` under `tech.kzen.lib.platform`. No such symbol exists in kzen-lib or kzen-auto source.
- **F7. `kzen/docs/CODING_STANDARDS.md` CC-21** gives `BuildInfo` as an example of the "reciprocal keep-in-sync markers" style. The kzen-auto copy (`kzen-auto-jvm/.../server/context/BuildInfo.kt:6-9`) names no counterpart. Only the launcher (`:8-10`) and shell (`:9-16`) copies point at the others, so the markers are not reciprocal.
- **F8. `kzen/docs/plans/2026-07-25_master-plan.md:76,278,305`** still show J5b as ☐ and say "Re-elaborate and run J5b next". `2026-07-25_job-improvements.md:377-426` records "J5b fixes 1–7 (2026-10-10)", and the HEAD commit `5998b7e` says "J5b fixes 1–7 (S4 at ~0.82 of Report)". The ledger is stale.

No sprint 3 exists (`docs/plans/` has only `sprint-1/` and `sprint-2/`).

Principle violation by design docs (true of the code, but against the user's stated rule).** `kzen/docs/analysis/2026-09-02_data-format-detection.md:412-418`, `kzen/docs/plans/data-format-detection/README.md` ("Status: complete", design authority), `kzen-auto/AGENTS.md:113` and `kzen-auto/docs/architecture.md:902-908` all prescribe content sniffing.
